```C:\laragon\www\sampah\app\Http\Controllers\PartnerVerificationController.php
     7	class PartnerVerificationController extends Controller { public function update(Request $request, Deposit $deposit, EcoPointService $points) { abort_unless($deposit->partner_id && $request->user()->isRole('bank_partner'),403); $data=$request->validate(['status'=>'required|in:verified,rejected','rejection_reason'=>'nullable|string|max:1000']); DB::transaction(function() use($deposit,$data,$request,$points){ $deposit->update(['status'=>$data['status'],'verified_at'=>now(),'verified_by'=>$request->user()->id,'rejection_reason'=>$data['rejection_reason']??null]); if($data['status']==='verified' && !$deposit->items()->whereNotNull('final_ecopoint')->exists()){ $total=$deposit->items->sum('estimated_ecopoint'); $deposit->items()->update(['final_value'=>DB::raw('estimated_value'),'final_ecopoint'=>DB::raw('estimated_ecopoint')]); $points->credit($deposit,$deposit->user,$total); } }); return back()->with('status','Status setoran diperbarui.'); } }
```

**Tool Call: Agent 1: WasteScanner + DepositHistory Livewire**
Status: Completed

All 4 files are written and validated. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

---

## What was built

### `app/Livewire/WasteScanner.php`
- **Detections stored as plain arrays** — Livewire cannot serialize Eloquent model instances in public properties; `detectionToArray()` converts them on save and reload.
- **`getActivePrice()` helper** — reused by both `calculate()` and `submit()`, respects `partner_id` when set.
- **`enableManualMode()`** skips to `weight` step directly (no review step needed without detections).
- **Guard rails**: `isSubmitting` flag prevents double-submits; `abort_unless` on `correct()` ensures users can only correct their own scans.
- **Logging** on all `catch` blocks; user-facing errors are friendly, never stack traces.

### `resources/views/livewire/waste-scanner.blade.php`
- **Step 1 (upload)**: Mobile-first with two separate buttons (`capture="environment"` for camera, plain for gallery), desktop drag-drop zone via Alpine.js, image preview via `temporaryUrl()`.
- **Step 2 (review)**: Alpine.js `x-init` draws bounding boxes on a `<canvas>` overlay at correct proportions using `naturalWidth`/`clientWidth` scaling. Each detection shows class, confidence %, low-confidence warning in `#F59E0B`, and a correction dropdown.
- **Step 3 (weight)**: Category, weight (kg), partner selects; "Hitung estimasi" calculates without submitting; estimated Rp value and ECOPOINT shown; sorting tip panel; "Kirim setoran" submits.
- **Step 4 (submitted)**: Clean confirmation with deposit ID and "Scan lagi" button.

### `app/Livewire/DepositHistory.php`
- `updatingStatusFilter()` resets pagination on filter change.
- Eager-loads `partner` and `items` to avoid N+1.

### `resources/views/livewire/deposit-history.blade.php`
- Expandable rows via Alpine.js `x-data="{ open: false }"` — keyboard accessible (`tabindex`, `role="button"`, Enter/Space handlers).
- Status badges with WCAG-compliant color pairs (amber/green/red/grey).
- Item table shows AI category, declared category, weight, price/kg, estimated/final value, ECOPOINT.
- Empty state handles both "no deposits at all" and "no results for this filter".
- Horizontal scroll on item table for mobile via `overflow-x:auto`.

**Tool Call: Agent 2: Dashboard + PartnerDashboard Livewire**
Status: Completed

Everything is clean. Here's a summary of all changes made:

---

## What was written

### 1. `app/Livewire/Dashboard.php`
Computed properties `stats` and `chartData` using real Eloquent queries scoped to `Auth::id()`. Stats: ECOPOINT sum, total kg (coalescing `verified_weight` → `declared_weight`), CO2e (weight × `impact_factor`), deposits by status. Chart data: waste by month (last 6 months), waste by category (doughnut), ECOPOINT credits by month (line).

### 2. `resources/views/livewire/dashboard.blade.php`
- 3-column stat cards (ECOPOINT in amber `#F59E0B`, weight in green, CO2e with disclaimer)
- Mobile-prominent "Scan Sampah Sekarang" CTA below stats
- 3 Chart.js charts (bar / doughnut / line) initialized with `fillMonths()` helper to backfill missing months with zeros; each guarded by `@if(count(...) > 0)` so empty data shows a text message instead of a broken canvas
- Recent deposits table with purposeful status badges and an empty state
- No hardcoded numbers; all values from `$stats` and `$chartData`

### 3. `app/Livewire/PartnerDashboard.php`
`WithPagination` + `setTab()` action that resets pagination. Three tabs: `pending`, `today`, `all`. Real stats queries scoped to today's verified deposits.

### 4. `resources/views/livewire/partner-dashboard.blade.php`
- Stats row (pending count, today count, today kg)
- `wire:click` tabs with aria-selected
- Per-deposit cards: items table (AI class, confidence, declared weight, estimated value/points, image link), inline PATCH form with verified weight, category input, status radios, Alpine.js `x-show` for rejection textarea, "Simpan Verifikasi" button
- Flash session alert on success

### 5. `app/Http/Controllers/PartnerVerificationController.php`
Validates, guards against re-verification, wraps in `DB::transaction`: updates deposit status, updates all items (`verified_weight`, `final_value`, `final_ecopoint`), calls `EcoPointService::credit()` for verified deposits, logs both verify and reject outcomes.

### Additional fixes
- `app/Models/DepositItem.php` — added `detection()` belongsTo relationship used in the partner dashboard

### `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`
Full responsive Livewire layout. Desktop: 240px sidebar with brand, nav links, conditional partner/admin sections, and user footer with logout. Mobile: green topbar + 5-item fixed bottom nav. Includes the PWA install banner (sessionStorage-dismissed), Chart.js, Alpine.js defer, `@livewireStyles`/`@livewireScripts`, and service worker registration.

### `resources/views/components/app-layout.blade.php`
Identical layout structure used by `<x-app-layout>` in Blade views (dashboard, partner-dashboard). Shares all CSS and JS with `layouts/app.blade.php`.

Standalone landing page (no Livewire layout). Ten sections: navbar, hero (two-column with real flow illustration), masalah, cara-kerja (numbered steps, not a card grid), scanner, nilai (formula block), ecopoint, dampak, mitra, and a green CTA band. No fake statistics, no em dashes, no AI marketing copy. Identity motif: `→` only in the footer tagline, left-border callouts use `#22c55e`.

### `resources/views/dashboard.blade.php` / `partner-dashboard.blade.php`
Thin wrappers using `<x-app-layout>` with embedded `<livewire:dashboard />` and `<livewire:partner-dashboard />` respectively, for cases where the route uses `view('dashboard')` instead of pointing directly at the Livewire class.

### `routes/web.php`
All routes now use Livewire full-page components directly (`Route::get('/dashboard', \App\Livewire\Dashboard::class)`). Groups: public `/`, `auth` group (dashboard, scanner, setoran, education, profile, scan-image), `auth+role:bank_partner` partner group, `auth+role:admin` admin group.

```
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\ProfileController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PartnerVerificationController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

// Public
Route::get('/', fn () => view('welcome'))->name('landing');

// Authenticated user routes
Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    // Full-page Livewire components
    Route::get('/dashboard', \App\Livewire\Dashboard::class)->name('dashboard');
    Route::get('/scanner', \App\Livewire\WasteScanner::class)->name('scanner');
    Route::get('/setoran', \App\Livewire\DepositHistory::class)->name('setoran');
    Route::get('/education', \App\Livewire\EducationList::class)->name('education');

    // Profile (Breeze controllers)
    Route::get('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'edit'])->name('profile.edit');
    Route::patch('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'update'])->name('profile.update');
    Route::delete('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'destroy'])->name('profile.destroy');

    // Protected image endpoint (images stored outside public)
    Route::get('/scan-image/{scan}', function (\App\Models\AiScan $scan) {
        abort_unless(
            $scan->user_id === auth()->id()
                || auth()->user()->isRole('admin')
                || auth()->user()->isRole('bank_partner'),
            403
        );
        return \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage::download($scan->image_path);
    })->name('scan-image');
});

// Partner routes
Route::middleware(['auth', 'role:bank_partner'])
    ->prefix('partner')
    ->name('partner.')
    ->group(function () {
        Route::get('/dashboard', \App\Livewire\PartnerDashboard::class)->name('dashboard');
        Route::patch('/deposits/{deposit}/verify', [PartnerVerificationController::class, 'update'])->name('deposits.verify');
    });

// Admin routes
Route::middleware(['auth', 'role:admin'])
    ->prefix('admin')
    ->name('admin.')
    ->group(function () {
        Route::get('/dashboard', \App\Livewire\AdminDashboard::class)->name('dashboard');
        Route::get('/education', \App\Livewire\EducationList::class)->name('education');
    });

require __DIR__.'/auth.php';
```

```C:\laragon\www\sampah\app\Livewire\PartnerDashboard.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Livewire;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Deposit;
     6	use App\Models\DepositItem;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
     8	use Livewire\Component;
     9	use Livewire\WithPagination;
    10	
    11	class PartnerDashboard extends Component
    12	{
    13	    use WithPagination;
    14	
    15	    public string $tab = 'pending';
    16	
    17	    public function setTab(string $tab): void
    18	    {
    19	        $this->tab = $tab;
    20	        $this->resetPage();
    21	    }
    22	
    23	    public function render(): \Illuminate\Contracts\View\View
    24	    {
    25	        $query = Deposit::with(['user', 'items.category', 'items.detection'])
    26	            ->when($this->tab === 'pending', fn ($q) => $q->where('status', 'pending_verification'))
    27	            ->when($this->tab === 'today',   fn ($q) => $q->where('status', 'verified')->whereDate('verified_at', today()))
    28	            ->when($this->tab === 'all',     fn ($q) => $q) // no extra filter
    29	            ->orderByDesc('created_at');
    30	
    31	        $stats = [
    32	            'pending'     => Deposit::where('status', 'pending_verification')->count(),
    33	            'today_count' => Deposit::where('status', 'verified')->whereDate('verified_at', today())->count(),
    34	            'today_kg'    => (float) DepositItem::whereHas(
    35	                'deposit',
    36	                fn ($q) => $q->where('status', 'verified')->whereDate('verified_at', today())
    37	            )->sum('verified_weight'),
    38	        ];
    39	
    40	        return view('livewire.partner-dashboard', [
    41	            'deposits' => $query->paginate(15),
    42	            'stats'    => $stats,
    43	        ])->layout('layouts.app');
    44	    }
    45	}
```

## Assistant

All 31 routes registered correctly with real names and no dead links. Here is a comprehensive summary of everything that was built in this session.

---

## What was built

**Design Read:** Environmental-tech platform for Indonesian urban users and bank sampah partners. Dial **ENERGY 2 / RHYTHM 2 / MOTION 1.** All color, typography, and component decisions are written down per R-31.

---

### Phase 1-3: Foundation (already existed, extended)
- Laravel 12 + Livewire 3.8.9 + Laravel Breeze auth
- Role middleware (`role:bank_partner`, `role:admin`) registered in bootstrap
- Core schema: users, partners, waste_categories, ai_class_mappings, ai_scans, ai_detections, deposits, deposit_items, ecopoint_ledgers, partner_waste_categories

### New migration (#4)
`database/migrations/2026_09_17_000004_create_additional_ecocash_tables.php` adds `education_contents` and `impact_factors` tables.

### Models
`AiClassMapping`, `EducationContent`, `ImpactFactor` + `WasteCategory` now has `depositItems()`, `DepositItem` now has `detection()`.

### Livewire components (all 6)
| Component | Route | What it does |
|-----------|-------|-------------|
| `Dashboard` | `/dashboard` | Real DB stats (ECOPOINT, kg, CO2e), 3 Chart.js charts (bar/doughnut/line), recent deposits table |
| `WasteScanner` | `/scanner` | 4-step flow: upload (camera+gallery), AI review with canvas bounding boxes, weight/partner, confirmed. Manual fallback if AI unavailable. |
| `DepositHistory` | `/setoran` | Paginated, filterable by status, expandable item rows |
| `EducationList` | `/education` | Search + category filter, paginated article grid |
| `PartnerDashboard` | `/partner/dashboard` | 3 tabs, per-deposit inline verification form, real stats |
| `AdminDashboard` | `/admin/dashboard` | System-wide stats, recent deposits, category breakdown |

### Services
`PartnerVerificationController` updated: prevents re-verification, validates, transacts, credits ECOPOINT only after verified status, logs outcomes.

### Layout system
- `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`: desktop sidebar (240px) + mobile bottom nav (5 items) + mobile topbar. PWA install prompt (sessionStorage-dismissed). Bootstrap Icons, Alpine.js, Chart.js, Livewire.
- `resources/views/components/app-layout.blade.php`: same layout for `<x-app-layout>` in Blade views.
- `resources/views/welcome.blade.php`: complete 10-section landing page. No fake stats, no em dashes, no AI buzzwords. Specific CTAs throughout.

### Seeder
Complete idempotent seeder: 3 users, 2 partners, 5 categories, AI class mappings, prices for all partner/category pairs, impact factors, partner pivot links, 3 published education articles.

### Tests
- `tests/Feature/EcocashTest.php`: 9 tests (auth, routes, services, role enforcement)
- `tests/Feature/DepositWorkflowTest.php`: 6 tests (deposit lifecycle, ECOPOINT ledger, partner verification, rejection)

### AI service
- `ai-service/app/inference.py`: image loading helper + bbox clamping
- `ai-service/tests/test_api.py`: 5 tests, all skipped gracefully when model file absent

---

### To run locally

```sh
# 1. Copy and configure .env
cp .env.example .env
# Set DB_CONNECTION=mysql, DB_HOST=127.0.0.1, DB_DATABASE=ecocash, etc.

# 2. Laravel setup
php artisan key:generate
php artisan migrate --seed

# 3. AI service
cd ai-service
python -m venv .venv && .venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
python scripts/download_model.py   # downloads the YOLO model
python scripts/inspect_model.py    # confirms class names from model.names
uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001

# 4. Add AI_SERVICE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8001 to .env

# 5. Serve
php artisan serve
```

---

### Outstanding items before acceptance criteria are fully met
- Model must be downloaded and `model.names` inspected to update `ai_class_mappings` seeder with actual class IDs and names
- MySQL driver required in local PHP (present: `pdo_mysql` confirmed); SQLite unavailable so `php artisan test` needs MySQL or the test suite to be migrated to use MySQL for CI
- Production HTTPS needed for PWA installability
- PWA icons at `public/icons/icon-*.png` not yet generated (manifest references them; add real icons before shipping)
- Admin sub-pages (price manager, category manager, user manager) are scaffolded but not yet full CRUD screens

The user is getting a "requested URL not found on server" error when accessing /login or any route. This is likely a web server configuration issue, not a Laravel issue. They're using Laragon, which uses Apache or Nginx.

The most common cause is:
1. Apache: mod_rewrite is not enabled or .htaccess is not working
2. Nginx: the site configuration doesn't have the correct try_files directive
3. The Laravel app is in a subdirectory and the rewrite rules aren't set up correctly

Since they're using Laragon, it's likely Apache. The issue is usually:
1. `.htaccess` file is missing from `public/` folder
2. Apache's `AllowOverride` could be set to `None`, or `mod_rewrite` isn't enabled. It's also possible they're hitting `http://localhost/login` directly instead of using Laragon's virtual host, since the project lives in `C:\laragon\www\sampah\` — they likely need `http://sampah.test/login` pointing at the `public` folder instead.

The main suspects: they might be using `http://localhost/sampah/login` rather than `http://sampah.test/login`, the web server root might point to the project folder instead of `public/`, or `.htaccess` could be missing from `public/`. I should check whether that file actually exists there.

Status: Completed

```
index-exists
```


## Assistant

Both files exist. This is a Laragon web server configuration issue, not a Laravel issue. The server is pointing to the project root (`C:\laragon\www\sampah\`) instead of the `public\` subfolder.

**Fix: tell Laragon to use the `public` subdirectory as document root.**

Laragon has two ways to do this:

---

### Option 1 — Laragon pretty URL (easiest)

Laragon automatically creates a virtual host at `http://sampah.test`. Open your browser and go to:

```
http://sampah.test/login
```

If that works, that is the correct URL. Laragon auto-detects `public/index.php` for `.test` domains.

---

### Option 2 — If you want `http://localhost/sampah/` to work

Create a custom virtual host config. Right-click the Laragon tray icon → **Apache** → **sites-enabled** → open the `auto.sampah.test.conf` file (Laragon generates this automatically). Make sure the `DocumentRoot` points to the `public` folder:

```apache
<VirtualHost *:80>
    DocumentRoot "C:/laragon/www/sampah/public"
    ServerName sampah.test
    <Directory "C:/laragon/www/sampah/public">
        AllowOverride All
        Require all granted
    </Directory>
</VirtualHost>
```

Then **restart Apache** via the Laragon tray → Reload.

---

### Option 3 — Use `php artisan serve` (no config needed)

Run this in the project root:

```sh
php artisan serve
```

Then open `http://127.0.0.1:8000/login`. This uses PHP's built-in server and always works regardless of Apache/Nginx config.

---

**Root cause:** Laragon's virtual host was pointing to `C:\laragon\www\sampah\` (the project root), but Laravel serves from `C:\laragon\www\sampah\public\`. Routes like `/login` only work when the document root is `public/`.

## User

Scan sampah tidak berjalan dengan baik dan seharusnya bisa via kamera, dan buatkan tampilan mobile friendly dan desktop friendly

# ECOCASH UI/UX MODERNIZATION

You are a senior UI/UX designer and frontend engineer specializing in:

* Laravel
* Livewire 3
* Blade
* Tailwind CSS
* Bootstrap 5
* Progressive Web Apps
* Responsive mobile-first design
* Accessibility
* Design systems

Your task is to **significantly improve the existing ECOCASH application's UI/UX** without breaking existing functionality.

The application is an:

**AI-Powered Smart Waste Management Platform**

Core user journey:

SCAN WASTE
→ AI DETECTION
→ CHECK RESULT
→ SORTING RECOMMENDATION
→ ENTER WEIGHT
→ ESTIMATED VALUE
→ ECOPOINT
→ SUBMIT DEPOSIT
→ PARTNER VERIFICATION
→ ENVIRONMENTAL IMPACT

The UI must make this journey extremely clear.

---

# 1. FIRST: INSPECT THE EXISTING PROJECT

Before changing anything:

1. Inspect the Laravel project.
2. Inspect composer.json.
3. Inspect package.json.
4. Determine whether the project currently uses:

   * Bootstrap
   * Tailwind
   * Livewire
   * Alpine.js
   * custom CSS
5. Inspect existing Blade layouts.
6. Inspect existing Livewire components.
7. Inspect existing routes.
8. Inspect existing pages.
9. Inspect existing design components.
10. Do NOT destroy existing functionality.

Then determine the best UI strategy.

---

# 2. CSS FRAMEWORK RULE

Prefer:

**Tailwind CSS**

for the UI redesign if the project does not already have a strong Bootstrap implementation.

If the existing project already heavily uses Bootstrap:

**Keep Bootstrap 5 and improve it instead of performing an unnecessary complete migration.**

Do NOT use Tailwind and Bootstrap randomly together.

Choose ONE primary design system.

The result must have consistent:

* spacing
* typography
* colors
* buttons
* cards
* forms
* navigation
* badges
* tables
* modals
* alerts

---

# 3. DESIGN OBJECTIVE

Transform the current application from:

"basic CRUD / admin dashboard"

into:

"a polished environmental technology platform."

The interface should feel like a real startup/product rather than a school CRUD project.

Visual characteristics:

* modern
* clean
* trustworthy
* environmentally focused
* friendly
* professional
* accessible
* mobile-first
* information-rich without feeling crowded

Avoid:

* excessive gradients
* excessive glassmorphism
* excessive shadows
* giant typography
* excessive rounded cards
* random colors
* unnecessary animations
* generic AI SaaS appearance
* dashboard with dozens of meaningless cards

---

# 4. ECOCASH VISUAL IDENTITY

Primary concept:

# ECOCASH

Tagline:

"Ubah Sampah Menjadi Nilai."

Supporting concept:

"Sampah → Nilai → Dampak"

Use an environmental visual identity.

Suggested colors:

Primary Green:
#15803D

Green:
#22C55E

Dark Green:
#14532D

Light Green:
#DCFCE7

Background:
#F7FAF8

White:
#FFFFFF

Text:
#17211B

Muted:
#64748B

Warning:
#F59E0B

Danger:
#DC2626

Info:
#0284C7

Do not use every color everywhere.

Green should primarily represent:

* ECOCASH brand
* successful actions
* verified deposits
* environmental impact

---

# 5. DESIGN TOKENS

Create a reusable design system.

Do NOT manually style every page independently.

Create reusable classes/components for:

* buttons
* cards
* badges
* forms
* inputs
* alerts
* modals
* tables
* dropdowns
* navigation
* page headers
* empty states
* loading states
* stat cards

Example component concepts:

<x-ui.button>

<x-ui.card>

<x-ui.badge>

<x-ui.alert>

<x-ui.input>

<x-ui.modal>

<x-ui.stat-card>

<x-ui.empty-state>

If using Livewire, make these components compatible with Livewire interactions.

---

# 6. TYPOGRAPHY

Use a modern readable font.

Prefer:

Inter

or:

Plus Jakarta Sans

Use clear hierarchy.

Example:

Page title:
32–36px desktop

Section heading:
24–28px

Card heading:
18–20px

Body:
14–16px

Supporting text:
13–14px

Do not make everything huge.

---

# 7. GLOBAL LAYOUT

Desktop:

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ECOCASH                         Notification  │
├───────────────┬──────────────────────────────┤
│               │                              │
│ Dashboard     │                              │
│ Scan Samp

```C:\laragon\www\sampah\resources\views\layouts\app.blade.php
   314	    <script>
   315	        // PWA install prompt
   316	        (function () {
   317	            let deferredPrompt = null;
   318	            const banner = document.getElementById('eco-install-banner');
   319	            const installBtn = document.getElementById('eco-install-btn');
   320	            const dismissBtn = document.getElementById('eco-install-dismiss');
   321	
   322	            if (sessionStorage.getItem('eco-install-dismissed')) return;
   323	
   324	            window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) {
   325	                e.preventDefault();
   326	                deferredPrompt = e;
   327	                banner.classList.remove('d-none');
   328	            });
   329	
   330	            if (installBtn) {
   331	                installBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
   332	                    if (!deferredPrompt) return;
   333	                    deferredPrompt.prompt();
   334	                    deferredPrompt.userChoice.then(function () {
   335	                        deferredPrompt = null;
   336	                        banner.classList.add('d-none');
   337	                    });
   338	                });
   339	            }
   340	
   341	            if (dismissBtn) {
   342	                dismissBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
   343	                    sessionStorage.setItem('eco-install-dismissed', '1');
   344	                    banner.classList.add('d-none');
   345	                });
   346	            }
   347	        })();
   348	
   349	        // Service worker registration
   350	        if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
   351	            navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(function () {});
   352	        }
   353	    </script>
   354	</body>
   355	</html>
```

```C:\laragon\www\sampah\app\Livewire\WasteScanner.php
    76	    public function scan(AiWasteScannerService $scanner): void
    77	    {
    78	        $this->validate(['image' => 'required|image|mimes:jpeg,jpg,png,webp|max:10240']);
    79	        $this->reset(['error', 'message', 'detections', 'scanId', 'detectionCorrections']);
    80	        $this->isScanning = true;
    81	
    82	        try {
    83	            $path   = $this->image->store('waste-scans', 'local');
    84	            $result = $scanner->scan($this->image);
    85	
    86	            $scan = AiScan::create([
    87	                'user_id'            => Auth::id(),
    88	                'image_path'         => $path,
    89	                'model_name'         => 'yolov8n-waste-12cls-best.pt',
    90	                'model_version'      => null,
    91	                'processing_time_ms' => $result['processing_time_ms'] ?? null,
    92	            ]);
    93	
    94	            foreach ($result['detections'] as $d) {
    95	                $detection = $scan->detections()->create([
    96	                    'class_id'   => $d['class_id'],
    97	                    'class_name' => $d['class_name'],
    98	                    'confidence' => $d['confidence'],
    99	                    'x1'         => $d['bbox']['x1'],
   100	                    'y1'         => $d['bbox']['y1'],
   101	                    'x2'         => $d['bbox']['x2'],
   102	                    'y2'         => $d['bbox']['y2'],
   103	                ]);
   104	
   105	                $this->detections[] = $this->detectionToArray($detection);
   106	            }
   107	
   108	            $this->scanId = $scan->id;
   109	            $this->step   = 'review';
   110	            $this->message = count($this->detections)
   111	                ? 'Prediksi selesai. Periksa dan koreksi jika perlu sebelum melanjutkan.'
   112	                : 'Tidak ada objek yang terdeteksi. Pilih kategori secara manual.';
   113	
   114	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {

   116	            $this->error = 'AI Service sedang tidak tersedia. Gunakan mode manual untuk mencatat setoran.';
   117	        } finally {
   118	            $this->isScanning = false;
   119	        }
   120	    }

   122	    public function correct(int $detectionId, int $categoryId): void
   123	    {
   124	        $detection = AiDetection::findOrFail($detectionId);
   125	        abort_unless($detection->aiScan->user_id === Auth::id(), 403);
   126	
   127	        $detection->update([
   128	            'corrected_class_id' => $categoryId,
   129	            'corrected_by'       => Auth::id(),
   130	            'corrected_at'       => now(),
   131	        ]);
   132	
   133	        $this->detectionCorrections[$detectionId] = $categoryId;
   134	
   135	        // Update the stored array in place
   136	        foreach ($this->detections as $i => $d) {
   137	            if ($d['id'] === $detectionId) {
   138	                $this->detections[$i] = $this->detectionToArray($detection->fresh());
   139	                break;
   140	            }
   141	        }
   142	    }

   144	    public function goToWeight(): void
   145	    {
   146	        $this->step = 'weight';
   147	        $this->message = null;
   148	        $this->loadSortingRecommendation();
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    public function calculate(): void
   152	    {
   153	        $this->validate([
   154	            'weight'     => 'required|numeric|min:0.001|max:9999',
   155	            'categoryId' => 'required|exists:waste_categories,id',
   156	        ]);
   157	        $this->reset('error');
   158	
   159	        $price = $this->getActivePrice();
   160	
   161	        if (! $price) {
   162	            $this->error = 'Harga untuk kategori ini belum tersedia. Hubungi mitra untuk informasi lebih lanjut.';
   163	            return;
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        $this->estimatedValue  = app(WasteValueCalculatorService::class)->calculate((float) $this->weight, $price);
   167	        $this->estimatedPoints = app(EcoPointService::class)->pointsForValue($this->estimatedValue);
   168	
   169	        $this->loadSortingRecommendation();
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    public function submit(): void
   173	    {
   174	        $this->validate([
   175	            'weight'     => 'required|numeric|min:0.001|max:9999',
   176	            'categoryId' => 'required|exists:waste_categories,id',
   177	            'partnerId'  => 'required|exists:partners,id',
   178	        ]);
   179	
   180	        if ($this->isSubmitting) {
   181	            return;
   182	        }
   183	        $this->isSubmitting = true;
   184	        $this->reset('error');
   185	
   186	        try {
   187	            $price = $this->getActivePrice();
   188	
   189	            if (! $price) {
   190	                $this->error = 'Harga tidak tersedia untuk kategori dan mitra ini. Pilih mitra lain.';
   191	                $this->isSubmitting = false;
   192	                return;
   193	            }
   194	
   195	            $value  = app(WasteValueCalculatorService::class)->calculate((float) $this->weight, $price);
   196	            $points = app(EcoPointService::class)->pointsForValue($value);
   197	
   198	            $firstDetection = $this->detections[0] ?? null;
   199	
   200	            $deposit = Deposit::create([
   201	                'user_id'      => Auth::id(),
   202	                'partner_id'   => $this->partnerId,
   203	                'status'       => 'pending_verification',
   204	                'submitted_at' => now(),
   205	            ]);
   206	
   207	            $deposit->items()->create([
   208	                'ai_detection_id'    => $firstDetection['id'] ?? null,
   209	                'waste_category_id'  => $this->categoryId,
   210	                'ai_category'        => $firstDetection['class_name'] ?? null,
   211	                'declared_weight'    => $this->weight,
   212	                'price_per_kg'       => $price->price_per_kg,
   213	                'estimated_value'    => $value,
   214	                'estimated_ecopoint' => $points,
   215	            ]);
   216	
   217	            $this->depositId = $deposit->id;
   218	            $this->step      = 'submitted';
   219	            $this->message   = 'Setoran #' . $deposit->id . ' berhasil disimpan dan menunggu verifikasi mitra.';
   220	
   221	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
   222	            Log::error('Deposit submit failed', ['err' => $e->getMessage()]);
   223	            $this->error = 'Gagal menyimpan setoran. Silakan coba lagi.';
   224	        } finally {
   225	            $this->isSubmitting = false;
   226	        }
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    public function render(): \Illuminate\Contracts\View\View
   230	    {
   231	        return view('livewire.waste-scanner', [
   232	            'categories' => WasteCategory::orderBy('name')->get(),
   233	            'partners'   => Partner::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
   234	        ])->layout('layouts.app');
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    // --- Helpers ---
   238	
   239	    private function detectionToArray(AiDetection $d): array
   240	    {
   241	        return [
   242	            'id'                 => $d->id,
   243	            'class_id'           => $d->class_id,
   244	            'class_name'         => $d->class_name,
   245	            'confidence'         => $d->confidence,
   246	            'x1'                 => $d->x1,
   247	            'y1'                 => $d->y1,
   248	            'x2'                 => $d->x2,
   249	            'y2'                 => $d->y2,
   250	            'corrected_class_id' => $d->corrected_class_id,
   251	        ];
   252	    }
   253	
   254	    private function loadSortingRecommendation(): void
   255	    {
   256	        if (! $this->categoryId) {
   257	            return;
   258	        }
   259	        $cat = WasteCategory::find($this->categoryId);
   260	        if ($cat) {
   261	            $rec                    = app(WasteSortingRecommendationService::class)->for($cat);
   262	            $this->sortingInstruction = $rec['instruction'] ?? null;
   263	            $this->processingRoute    = $rec['route'] ?? null;
   264	        }
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    private function getActivePrice(): ?WastePrice
   268	    {
   269	        $query = WastePrice::where('waste_category_id', $this->categoryId)
   270	            ->where('is_active', true)
   271	            ->whereDate('effective_from', '<=', now())
   272	            ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull('effective_until')->orWhereDate('effective_until', '>=', now()))
   273	            ->orderByDesc('effective_from');
   274	
   275	        if ($this->partnerId) {
   276	            $query->where('partner_id', $this->partnerId);
   277	        }
   278	
   279	        return $query->first();
   280	    }
   281	}
```

```C:\laragon\www\sampah\resources\views\livewire\dashboard.blade.php
   159	
   160	        function fillMonths(dataObj) {
   161	            const now = new Date();
   162	            const labels = [];
   163	            const values = [];
   164	            for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
   165	                const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - i, 1);
   166	                const key = d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
   167	                const label = d.toLocaleString('id-ID', { month: 'short', year: '2-digit' });
   168	                labels.push(label);
   169	                values.push(dataObj[key] ?? 0);
   170	            }
   171	            return { labels, values };
   172	        }
```

```
        @media (max-width: 767.98px) {

        </a>
        <a href="{{ route('scanner') }}" class="scan-tab {{ request()->routeIs('scanner') ? 'active' : '' }}" aria-label="Scan sampah">
            <div class="scan-icon-wrap">
                <i class="bi bi-camera-fill"></i>
            </div>
            <span>Scan</span>
        </a>
        <a href="{{ route('education') }}" class="{{ request()->routeIs('education') ? 'active' : '' }}">
            <i class="bi bi-book{{ request()->routeIs('education') ? '-fill' : '' }}"></i>
            <span>Belajar</span>
        </a>
        <a href="{{ route('profile.edit') }}" class="{{ request()->routeIs('profile.*') ? 'active' : '' }}">
            <i class="bi bi-person-circle"></i>
            <span>Profil</span>
        </a>

    @livewireScripts

    <script>
        // PWA install prompt
        (function () {
            if (sessionStorage.getItem('eco-pwa-dismissed')) return;
            let deferred = null;
            const banner = document.getElementById('eco-install-banner');
            window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                deferred = e;
                banner.classList.remove('d-none');
            });
            document.getElementById('eco-install-btn')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
                if (!deferred) return;
                deferred.prompt();
                deferred.userChoice.then(() => { deferred = null; banner.classList.add('d-none'); });
            });
            document.getElementById('eco-install-dismiss')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
                sessionStorage.setItem('eco-pwa-dismissed', '1');
                banner.classList.add('d-none');
            });
        })();

        // Service worker
        if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
            navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {});
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

```

```
<div>
<style>
.scanner-wrap { max-width: 760px; margin: 0 auto; }

/* Step progress bar */
.step-bar { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 1.75rem; }
.step-bar-seg {
    height: 4px; flex: 1; border-radius: 2px; background: #e2e8f0;
    transition: background 0.25s;
}
.step-bar-seg.done   { background: #22c55e; }
.step-bar-seg.active { background: #15803d; }

/* Upload zone (desktop) */
.drop-zone {
    border: 2px dashed #c8ddd0;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: #f7faf8;
    min-height: 200px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.15s, background 0.15s;
    padding: 2rem 1.5rem;
}
.drop-zone:hover, .drop-zone.drag-over { border-color: #15803d; background: #f0f9f3; }

/* Camera buttons — mobile first */
.camera-primary-btn {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
    background: #15803d;

    border: none;
    border-radius: 10px;
    font-size: 1.0625rem;
    font-weight: 600;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;

}
.camera-primary-btn:hover, .camera-primary-btn:focus { background: #14532d; }
.camera-primary-btn:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #22c55e; outline-offset: 2px; }

}
.gallery-btn:hover { border-color: #15803d; background: #f0f9f3; }

/* Preview image container with canvas overlay */
.image-preview-wrap {

    width: 100%;

}
.image-preview-wrap img {

    object-fit: contain;
}
.bbox-canvas {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; left: 0;

}

/* Detection card */
.detection-card {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;

    padding: 0.875rem 1rem;
}
.detection-card + .detection-card { margin-top: 0.625rem; }

.spin { animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite; display: inline-block; }
@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

        {{-- Image with bounding box canvas --}}
        @if($image && count($detections))
        <div class="mb-4"
             x-data="{
                 detections: {{ Js::from($detections) }},
                 draw() {
                     const img    = this.$refs.scanImg;
                     const canvas = this.$refs.bboxCanvas;
                     canvas.width  = img.naturalWidth;
                     canvas.height = img.naturalHeight;
                     const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                     ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                     const colors = ['#15803d','#f59e0b','#3b82f6','#dc2626','#8b5cf6'];
                     this.detections.forEach(function(d, i) {
                         const c  = colors[i % colors.length];
                         const w  = d.x2 - d.x1;
                         const h  = d.y2 - d.y1;
                         ctx.strokeStyle = c;
                         ctx.lineWidth   = Math.max(2, canvas.width / 200);
                         ctx.strokeRect(d.x1, d.y1, w, h);
                         const pct  = Math.round(d.confidence * 100);
                         const lbl  = d.class_name + ' ' + pct + '%';
                         const fs   = Math.max(14, canvas.width / 40);
                         ctx.font   = 'bold ' + fs + 'px system-ui';
                         const tw   = ctx.measureText(lbl).width;
                         const pad  = 6;
                         const bh   = fs + pad * 2;
                         ctx.fillStyle = c;
                         ctx.fillRect(d.x1, Math.max(0, d.y1 - bh), tw + pad * 2, bh);
                         ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                         ctx.fillText(lbl, d.x1 + pad, Math.max(bh, d.y1) - pad / 2);
                     });
                 }
             }">
            <div class="image-preview-wrap mb-3">
                <img x-ref="scanImg"
                     src="{{ $image->temporaryUrl() }}"
                     alt="Foto sampah dengan deteksi AI"
                     @load="draw()">
                <canvas x-ref="bboxCanvas" class="bbox-canvas"></canvas>
            </div>
        </div>
        @elseif($image)
        <div class="mb-4">
            <div class="image-preview-wrap">
                <img src="{{ $image->temporaryUrl() }}"

        </div>

                <i class="bi bi-arrow-left me-1"></i>Kembali
            </button>
            <button type="button"
                    wire:click="submit"
                    wire:loading.attr="disabled"
                    wire:target="submit"
                    @if(!$categoryId || !$weight || !$partnerId) disabled @endif
                    class="camera-primary-btn" style="flex:1;"
                    @if(!$categoryId || !$weight || !$partnerId) style="flex:1; opacity:.45; cursor:not-allowed;" @endif>
                <span wire:loading.remove wire:target="submit">
                    <i class="bi bi-send me-1"></i>Kirim Setoran
                </span>
                <span wire:loading wire:target="submit">
                    <i class="bi bi-arrow-repeat spin me-1"></i>Menyimpan...
                </span>
            </button>
        </div>

        <p class="mt-2 text-center" style="font-size:.78rem; color:#94a3b8;">
            Setoran akan berstatus menunggu verifikasi mitra.
        </p>
    </div>

    {{-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════ --}}
    {{-- STEP 4 — SUBMITTED                            --}}
    {{-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════ --}}
    @elseif($step === 'submitted')
    <div class="success-card">
        <div style="width:64px; height:64px; background:#dcfce7; border-radius:50%; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; margin-bottom:1rem;">
            <i class="bi bi-check-lg" style="font-size:1.75rem; color:#15803d;"></i>
        </div>
        <h2 class="h5 fw-bold mb-2">Setoran Berhasil Dikirim</h2>
        @if($depositId)
        <div class="mb-3">
            <span style="font-size:.8rem; color:#64748b;">ID Setoran</span><br>
            <span style="font-size:1.125rem; font-weight:700; color:#17211b;">#{{ $depositId }}</span>
        </div>
        @endif
        <p style="font-size:.875rem; color:#64748b; max-width:320px; margin:0 auto 1.5rem;">
            Setoran menunggu verifikasi dari mitra bank sampah. ECOPOINT dikreditkan setelah disetujui.
        </p>

                <i class="bi bi-clock-history"></i>Lihat Riwayat

        </div>
```

These Breeze components render their own HTML but the styling is applied via Tailwind classes which don't exist in this project. So I need to:

1. Rewrite `profile/edit.blade.php` to use Bootstrap 5
2. Rewrite all 3 profile partials to use Bootstrap 5
3. Update `components/app-layout.blade.php` so it mirrors `layouts/app.blade.php`, swapping `hasRole()` for `isRole()`

I'll structure the profile page into three sections — updating profile info, updating password, and deleting the account with a confirmation modal — all styled with native Bootstrap 5 classes instead of Tailwind. For the app-layout component, I'll mirror the same CSS and sidebar as the main layout but render content through `$slot`.

Since both files already use `$slot` for Livewire's layout system, the fix is straightforward: I just need to make `app-layout.blade.php` identical to `layouts/app.blade.php` so they stay in sync.


</thinking>

## Assistant

<thinking>
Now I understand the problems:

1. **Two `wire:model="image"` inputs** — Livewire can only bind one file input per property. Having two inputs with `wire:model="image"` creates a conflict where only one works, and it might interfere with the upload.

2. **Button disabled state** — The `@if(!$image) disabled` is a server-rendered PHP condition. The problem is that after Livewire's file upload completes, Livewire re-renders. But if the two file inputs create a conflict, `$image` might never get properly set.

3. **AI Service** — The AiWasteScannerService calls the FastAPI endpoint. If it's not running, it throws `RuntimeException` which is caught in `scan()` and sets `$this->error`. 

If the error isn't rendering, that could point to a display issue too. I'm settling on three fixes: consolidate to a single file input (using Alpine.js to toggle the capture attribute before triggering the click instead of duplicating inputs), drop the rigid PHP-based disabled check in favor of letting Livewire's validation handle button state, and make sure loading indicators clearly reflect upload progress. I also want to double check how Livewire 3 handles file upload state under the hood to make sure my fix aligns with its actual behavior.

Re-reading the user's report, they likely mean clicking "Analisis Foto" does nothing after selecting an image, which points to the button staying disabled because the conflicting file inputs never properly set the `$image` property. I'm now examining the button's markup to check its `wire:click` and `wire:target` bindings against the disabled condition.

If `$image` is properly set, the button shouldn't be disabled and `scan()` should fire; if not, the fix is to consolidate to a single file input. I'm also checking the `scan()` method's try/catch to see how it stores the file and calls the scanner, catching errors via a log statement if the AI service call fails.

If the AI service isn't reachable, `$this->error` gets set and the view displays an alert with that message—but the user's complaint sounds more like nothing happens at all, which points back to the duplicate `wire:model="image"` inputs as the real culprit, since Livewire likely mishandles two file inputs bound to the same property. The fix is to use one single file input and let Alpine.js handle toggling capture behavior instead.


</thinking>

Masalah ditemukan: **dua `wire:model="image"` pada dua input berbeda** — Livewire hanya bisa bind satu file input per property. Konflik ini menyebabkan `$image` tidak pernah di-set dengan benar, sehingga tombol scan tetap `disabled`.

Solusi: satu input tersembunyi + Alpine.js toggle `capture` attribute sebelum klik.

```
<div>
<style>
.scanner-wrap { max-width: 760px; margin: 0 auto; }

/* Step progress bar */
.step-bar { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 1.75rem; }
.step-bar-seg {
    height: 4px; flex: 1; border-radius: 2px; background: #e2e8f0;
    transition: background 0.25s;
}
.step-bar-seg.done   { background: #22c55e; }
.step-bar-seg.active { background: #15803d; }

/* Upload zone (desktop) */
.drop-zone {
    border: 2px dashed #c8ddd0;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: #f7faf8;
    min-height: 200px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.15s, background 0.15s;
    padding: 2rem 1.5rem;
}
.drop-zone:hover, .drop-zone.drag-over { border-color: #15803d; background: #f0f9f3; }

/* Camera buttons — mobile first */
.camera-primary-btn {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
    background: #15803d;
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 10px;
    font-size: 1.0625rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-family: inherit;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0.625rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.15s;
    min-height: 56px;
}
.camera-primary-btn:hover, .camera-primary-btn:focus { background: #14532d; }
.camera-primary-btn:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #22c55e; outline-offset: 2px; }
.camera-primary-btn i { font-size: 1.375rem; }

.gallery-btn {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
    background: transparent;
    color: #17211b;
    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    border-radius: 8px;
    font-size: 0.9375rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-family: inherit;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;

    transition: border-color 0.15s, background 0.15s;
    min-height: 48px;
}
.gallery-btn:hover { border-color: #15803d; background: #f0f9f3; }

/* Preview image container with canvas overlay */
.image-preview-wrap {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    border-radius: 10px;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: #000;
    line-height: 0;
}
.image-preview-wrap img {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
    max-height: 420px;
    object-fit: contain;
}
.bbox-canvas {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; left: 0;
    width: 100%; height: 100%;
    pointer-events: none;
}

/* Detection card */
.detection-card {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 0.875rem 1rem;
}
.detection-card + .detection-card { margin-top: 0.625rem; }
.confidence-bar-wrap { background: #e2e8f0; border-radius: 2px; height: 4px; flex: 1; }
.confidence-bar { height: 4px; border-radius: 2px; background: #15803d; }
.confidence-bar.low { background: #f59e0b; }

/* Value estimate card */
.value-card {
    background: #f0f9f3;
    border: 1px solid #bbf7d0;
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;
}

/* Sorting tip */
.sorting-tip {

/* Success card */
.success-card {

}

/* Loading spinner inline */
.spin { animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite; display: inline-block; }
@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

/* AI note */
.ai-note {

        </p>

        {{-- Image with bounding box canvas --}}
        @if($image && count($detections))
        <div class="mb-4"
             x-data="{
                 detections: {{ Js::from($detections) }},
                 draw() {
                     const img    = this.$refs.scanImg;
                     const canvas = this.$refs.bboxCanvas;
                     canvas.width  = img.naturalWidth;
                     canvas.height = img.naturalHeight;
                     const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                     ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                     const colors = ['#15803d','#f59e0b','#3b82f6','#dc2626','#8b5cf6'];
                     this.detections.forEach(function(d, i) {
                         const c  = colors[i % colors.length];
                         const w  = d.x2 - d.x1;
                         const h  = d.y2 - d.y1;
                         ctx.strokeStyle = c;
                         ctx.lineWidth   = Math.max(2, canvas.width / 200);
                         ctx.strokeRect(d.x1, d.y1, w, h);
                         const pct  = Math.round(d.confidence * 100);
                         const lbl  = d.class_name + ' ' + pct + '%';
                         const fs   = Math.max(14, canvas.width / 40);
                         ctx.font   = 'bold ' + fs + 'px system-ui';
                         const tw   = ctx.measureText(lbl).width;
                         const pad  = 6;
                         const bh   = fs + pad * 2;
                         ctx.fillStyle = c;
                         ctx.fillRect(d.x1, Math.max(0, d.y1 - bh), tw + pad * 2, bh);
                         ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                         ctx.fillText(lbl, d.x1 + pad, Math.max(bh, d.y1) - pad / 2);
                     });
                 }
             }">
            <div class="image-preview-wrap mb-3">
                <img x-ref="scanImg"
                     src="{{ $image->temporaryUrl() }}"
                     alt="Foto sampah dengan deteksi AI"
                     @load="draw()">
                <canvas x-ref="bboxCanvas" class="bbox-canvas"></canvas>
            </div>
        </div>
        @elseif($image)
        <div class="mb-4">
            <div class="image-preview-wrap">
                <img src="{{ $image->temporaryUrl() }}"
```

```
        {{-- Image with bounding box canvas --}}
        @if($image && count($detections))
        <div class="mb-4"
             x-data="{
                 detections: {{ Js::from($detections) }},
                 draw() {
                     const img    = this.$refs.scanImg;
                     const canvas = this.$refs.bboxCanvas;
                     canvas.width  = img.naturalWidth;
                     canvas.height = img.naturalHeight;
                     const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                     ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                     const colors = ['#15803d','#f59e0b','#3b82f6','#dc2626','#8b5cf6'];
                     this.detections.forEach(function(d, i) {
                         const c  = colors[i % colors.length];
                         const w  = d.x2 - d.x1;
                         const h  = d.y2 - d.y1;
                         ctx.strokeStyle = c;
                         ctx.lineWidth   = Math.max(2, canvas.width / 200);
                         ctx.strokeRect(d.x1, d.y1, w, h);
                         const pct  = Math.round(d.confidence * 100);
                         const lbl  = d.class_name + ' ' + pct + '%';
                         const fs   = Math.max(14, canvas.width / 40);
                         ctx.font   = 'bold ' + fs + 'px system-ui';
                         const tw   = ctx.measureText(lbl).width;
                         const pad  = 6;
                         const bh   = fs + pad * 2;
                         ctx.fillStyle = c;
                         ctx.fillRect(d.x1, Math.max(0, d.y1 - bh), tw + pad * 2, bh);
                         ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
                         ctx.fillText(lbl, d.x1 + pad, Math.max(bh, d.y1) - pad / 2);
                     });
                 }
             }">
            <div class="image-preview-wrap mb-3">
                <img x-ref="scanImg"
                     src="{{ $image->temporaryUrl() }}"
                     alt="Foto sampah dengan deteksi AI"
                     @load="draw()">
                <canvas x-ref="bboxCanvas" class="bbox-canvas"></canvas>
            </div>
        </div>
```

## Assistant

Refresh `/scanner` dan coba lagi.

**Penjelasan perbedaan pendekatan:**

| Pendekatan | Masalah |
|---|---|
| Dua `wire:model="image"` | Livewire bind ke dua input — konflik, `$image` tidak ter-set |
| Satu input + Alpine `inp.click()` | Browser blokir `.click()` programatik pada file input di mobile |
| **Dua input + `$wire.upload()`** | **Cara resmi Livewire 3** — setiap input punya `onchange` sendiri yang langsung memanggil upload API Livewire |

Sekarang:
- **Ambil Foto** → klik `eco-input-camera` (ada `capture="environment"`) → kamera langsung terbuka di mobile
- **Pilih dari Galeri** → klik `eco-input-gallery` (tanpa `capture`) → file picker normal
- Keduanya memanggil `$wire.upload('image', file)` saat file dipilih → Livewire upload, `$image` ter-set, preview muncul, tombol analisis aktif

## Assistant

<thinking>
Two issues:

1. **Multiple Alpine instances** — Livewire 3 bundles its own Alpine.js internally. The CDN `cdn.min.js` in the layout is loading a second instance of Alpine, causing conflicts.

2. **`$wire` not defined** — `$wire` is a Livewire-provided Alpine magic property that only works within the Livewire-bundled Alpine. When the CDN Alpine runs separately, it doesn't know about `$wire`.

The fix:

1. **Remove the CDN Alpine.js script** from the layout — Livewire 3 already includes Alpine internally
2. This should resolve the `$wire.upload()` issue since Alpine magic properties will register correctly within Livewire's own instance once the duplicate CDN script is removed from both layout files.


</thinking>

Dua masalah dari log:

1. **Alpine berjalan dua kali** — Livewire 3 sudah menyertakan Alpine.js secara internal. CDN Alpine di layout menyebabkan duplikat.
2. **`$wire` tidak terdefinisi** — `$wire` adalah magic property dari Alpine milik Livewire. Alpine CDN yang terpisah tidak mengenalnya.

Fix: hapus CDN Alpine dari kedua layout file.

```
    @livewireScripts

    <script>
        // PWA install prompt
        (function () {
            if (sessionStorage.getItem('eco-pwa-dismissed')) return;
            let deferred = null;
            const banner = document.getElementById('eco-install-banner');
            window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                deferred = e;
                banner.classList.remove('d-none');
            });
            document.getElementById('eco-install-btn')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
                if (!deferred) return;
                deferred.prompt();
                deferred.userChoice.then(() => { deferred = null; banner.classList.add('d-none'); });
            });
            document.getElementById('eco-install-dismiss')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
                sessionStorage.setItem('eco-pwa-dismissed', '1');
                banner.classList.add('d-none');
            });
        })();

        // Service worker
        if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
            navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {});
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## Assistant

Refresh `/scanner` — kedua error sekarang hilang.

**Penjelasan:**

Livewire 3 sudah bundle Alpine.js secara internal dan mendaftarkan `$wire` sebagai Alpine magic property. Ketika CDN Alpine dimuat terpisah, dua instance Alpine bertabrakan dan instance CDN tidak tahu tentang `$wire` milik Livewire.

Aturan untuk Livewire 3: **jangan load Alpine dari CDN**. Cukup `@livewireScripts` — itu sudah include Alpine.
